4-27-2012

Version 316
3-22-2012
1) Task_SaveSettings object modified to accurately display status info and also delete redundant action list from file object Save_Current_Configuration.
2) Reduce MessageDelay from 2 sec to 1 sec in action list SaveData to speed up autorecord process.
3) Many changes to Task_Adjust to correctly scale offset of data and Phase Wand for x axis compression, Phase Wand v
4) Course Balance modified so that A/D range is no longer visible, and gain range is fixed at +/-5 so that signal clipping is less common.
5) Tasks for continuous erase and block erase were combined into a single task named TaskXY_Dot.
6) Checkbox 2-Ch was addded to the strip chart to allow Task_Adjust to display just the two vertical (Y) channels.
7) Indentifying labels for the stripcharts were change from X and Y to H & V.

Version 315
8-28-2011
1) Main addition was the ability to record data using either a footswitch or using auto-record which recognizes the end of the tube.

12-28-2008
Version 313

1)  'Save and Quit' task list now calls DLL FlashLED to check for USB communications, and if error then don't save last.cfg, just halt application. 
2)  Put the 'Save Data' button back.

12-20-2008
Version 312

1)  Resize XY charts to be square and maximize size for 1280 x 800 wide screen display.
2)  Make stripchart channel identifying text white.
3)  Make all gain and phase and axis compression controls available from the 1XY display mode (Big XY chart).
4)  Add Tubelist Index slider to allow quick indexing through the tube list.
5)  Change Display Mode from drop down to buttons for quicker selection of display mode.
6)  Made Clip Data sticky so that data is always clipped at the completion of a stripchart if the box is selected. Unselecting
7)  Remove TaskAdjust flag from LoadConfigurations action list and Charts action list so that TaskAdjust does not run unt
8)  Rename 'Exit' to 'Save and Quit'.
9)  Add 'Quit' to allow user to exit program without saving settings and corrupting the last.cfg file in the event that the USB
10) Modify initializing routine so that the program defaults to 12kHz/3kHz differential/cross-axial when no last.cfg file is found.

6-6-2008
VERSION 311
Changes that affect this release include:

1)         Ch1-XY axis scale compression object X1 compressed the Y scale instead of the X scale. This has been corrected.
2)         The X axis scale compression no longer changes the X axis range of the XY plots while in the 2XY-1SC chart mod
 compression setting.  The range will always be from -5 to +5. This is consistent with the 1XY chart mode.
3)         The strip chart’s y scale was changed to give each channel in the strip chart a range of -5 to +5.  Since there are 4
now -20 to +20.
4)         The ‘Clip Data’ object was added to the strip chart to keep data from one channel from overlapping adjacent chan
Click it a second time to restore the full range of the data.
5)         The ‘Balance’ object is now enabled while running in the 1XY chart mode (except when in the fixed-time erase mo
stream to re-balance.
6)         A new window has been added to select color preferences for the 2XY-1SC chart mode.  The panel is accessed u
The ‘Grids’ object in this window is used for all charts in the 2XY-1SC mode, and in the 1XY mode.  The ‘1XY Setup’ is stil
 preferences for the 1XY chart mode, and for the 1XY chart auto-erase preferences.
7)         A new file, c:\ec14\mycolors.cfg, will be created the first time you exit the new version of the program.  This file is used to retain the settings last

VERSION 310
Disabled all controls except for STOP when the RUN control is actuated.
Make leading dot visible for Bulk Erase mode when time is 5 or less.
Change order of Strip Chart signasl top to bottom Ch1-Y, Ch1-X, Ch2-Y, Ch2-X.
Allow Task Initialize to run in the event that last.cfg and ect.lst files do not exist.

1-09-2008
version 309
Changed ch1 back to x and ch0 to y.  This is the only way to make x lead y in mathematical phase.
Also changed the math object calculations F1 Radians and F2 Radians  (360-deg) where deg= the Phase slider object.
Calculate F1 Radians and F2 Radians on loading of .cfg file.
Changed Phase Wand so that it rotates clockwise.

Version 3.02
Convert BigXY to USB1408

Version 3.01
Convert SPI to USB1408 (was still using LJU12 with the synch function for SPI)

Version 3.00
Convert stripcharting to USB1408

Version 2.00
Convert to LabJack U3
Startup button now does both the OpenLabJack, and configures streaming.
Strip chart legend

Version 1.05 a-i
Continued tweaking things but still using the U12
Flying dot on Big X-Y
phase wand
one soft gain control for both x and y gains

Version 1.05
Added the second channel frequency and gain adjustments.

SPI CS values are:
CS=0 -> FREQ 1 QUAD PHASE AD9833
CS=1 -> FREQ 1 IN-PHASE AD9833
CS=2 -> FREQ 2 IN-PHASE AD9833
CS=3 -> FREQ 2 QUAD PHASE AD9833
CS=4 -> FREQ 2 GAIN LTC6912
CS=5 -> FREQ 1 GAIN LTC6912

Version 1.04
Modified main screen to display one strip chart and 2 XY charts.

Balance (\EC14 CHARTS\Balance)

This object is used to calculate a  new 'avg' which is used to center
the strip chart.  If the balance has drifted out of the normal range, 
a card adjust loop runs.

Foot switch input is NOT used by this loop, so task 'foot' continues to 
run while this action list executes.

1)  Balance acquires 100 samples and computes the average value.  If
the average is out of the range of 1 to 3 volts, then the hall voltage
is displayed in 'Card Adjust' and the 'Done' button
is made visible.  The task enters a loop, 'a/d loop', which samples and
displays the hall voltage in real time to allow adjustment.  The loop
terminates when 'Done' is pressed.

When 'Adjust complete' is pressed, the 'card adjust' and 'calibration 
complete' objects are made non-visible, and the instructions display
'press balance' and the task list ends.

If the probe is balanced within range, the instructions display to 'press
switch - pull probe', and the task list ends.

Action list for Pushbutton object "Balance":

1) ------------------ ------------------------------------"-I-f- -'Stop Task' is false, then a task is running while
Balance was Pushed"

2) ------------------ ------------------------------------"-S--t-op the task,and set the Restart Flag."
3) If/Then x=y with x=StopTask y=0 
4) Store in StopTask from 1
5) If/Then x=y with x=Erase Mode y=0 
6) Stop TaskXY_Dot  
7) End If x=y
8) If/Then x=y with x=Erase Mode y=1 
9) Stop TaskXY_Dot  

10) End If x=y
11) Call cbStopBackground with BoardNum,1
12) Call cbWinBufFree with DataHandle
13) Store in RestartFlag from 1
14) End If x=y
15) ------------------ ------------------------------------"-G--e- t about 1000 points to use as the avg to

balance"
16) ------------------ ------------------------------------"-B--a-sically the same action list as 'Test Analog', but

without the display"
17) ------------------ ------------------------------------"-N--u- mSamples is 1984 based upon 4*31*16"
18) Calculate Vector of Zeros with x=NumSamples 
19) Store in RawData from Vector of Zeros
20) Clear Ch 0 Data
21) Clear Ch 1 Data
22) Clear Ch 2 Data
23) Clear Ch 3 Data
24) ------------------ ------------------------------------"-I-n--itialize 1408 function arguments"
25) Store in Status from 0
26) Store in FunctionType from 1
27) Store in BoardNum from 0
28) Store in LowChannel from 0
29) Store in HighChannel from 3

Save Data (\EC14 CHARTS\Save Data)

This object will cause the file object 'Select Directory' action list to
execute.  It does not allow for changing the Directory.  Use the
'Select Directory' button to change directories.

2) Set filename of Save Strip Chart File to data file name
3) Calculate FileStatusDat with file=data file name

Save Image (\EC14 CHARTS\Save Image)

Action list for Pushbutton object "Save Image":

1) Calculate image file name with zone=Zone row=Row  tube=Tube 
2) ------------------ M&C "connect to the COM server"
3) Create object SnagIt program object type="SnagIt.ImageCapture.1"
4) ------------------ M&C "set input mode (1=window)"
5) Set property SnagIt program name="Input" to value 1
6) ------------------ M&C "set input window to active window"
7) Set property SnagIt program name="InputWindowOptions.SelectionMethod" to value 1
8) ------------------ M&C "set output mode (2=file)"

task SaveSettings (\EC14 CHARTS\task SaveSettings)

This action performs a global exit from the application.  The action
list executes from the button 'Exit'.  This gives a more polished
ending to the runtime than just closing the window.

It also saves the defect list to a new file name.

TaskSC (\EC14 CHARTS\TaskSC)

This is the main application loop.  It doesn't multitask except when the 'Balance'
button is pressed when in the 'Test' mode.

The task can be started by the mode switch 'Test Mode' or by task 'trim'.  If 
the mode switch is in 'calibrate', then the task ends when a calibration tube
has been pulled.  The task also ends if the hall balance voltage needs to
be adjusted while in the 'calibrate' mode.

If in the 'Test' mode, the task ends when the mode is changed to 'Calibrate'
or the application is closed.

Action list for Task object "TaskSC":

TaskXY_FixedNoDot (\EC14 CHARTS\TaskXY_FixedNoDot)

This is the main application loop.  It doesn't multitask except when the 'Balance'
button is pressed when in the 'Test' mode.

The task can be started by the mode switch 'Test Mode' or by task 'trim'.  If 
the mode switch is in 'calibrate', then the task ends when a calibration tube
has been pulled.  The task also ends if the hall balance voltage needs to
be adjusted while in the 'calibrate' mode.

If in the 'Test' mode, the task ends when the mode is changed to 'Calibrate'
or the application is closed.

Action list for Task object "TaskXY_FixedNoDot":

Record (\EC14 CHARTS\Record)

Auto Recording is used to detect if the probe is in free space by doing the following
1) The user is instructed to balance the probe in free-air
2) The Balance action-list was modified to check a flag named AR_First_Flag that if set will save the balance values to FreeAir rather than Avg
3) Then the streaming routine TaskXY_Block_Dot was modified to create a list of the first value of each new data vector, and then compute Range.
4) TaskXY_Block_Dot was also modified to append all data to container A/D data list.
5) If Range is true then the Stop buttton is pushed, Next is pushed, and then Start is pushed.

Action list for Selector object "Record":

1) If/Then x=y with x=Record y=1 
2) Set Status - Strip Chart to "Position Probe in Free Space - Press Balance"
3) Store in AR_First_Flag from 1
4) End If x=y

Settings for Selector object "Record":

# Values: 3
Current Label Set: 
Labels: Off,Auto,Foot Switch
Values: 0,1,2
Label Set Names: 
Visible: 1
Enabled: 1
Exec. actions at initialize: 0
Initial Value: 0
BG Color: Ambient(panel)
Bezel Type: Both

Mode (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Mode)

mode 0  diffferential/cross axial or differential/differential - single frequency 
S1 ON  = BIT 3
S2 OFF
K1 ON  = BIT 5
K2 OFF
K3 OFF

mode 1  differential/absolute - dual frequency
S1 OFF
S2 ON = BIT 4
K1 OFF
K2 ON = BIT 6
K3 OFF

mode 2  send/receive - single frequency
S1 ON = BIT 3
S2 OFF
K1 OFF
K2 OFF
K3 ON = BIT 7

mode 3  differential/differential - dual frequency
S1 OFF
S2 OFF
K1 OFF
K2 ON = BIT 6
K3 OFF

Action list for Selector object "Mode":

1) ------------------ ---------SPI "Initialize Port B values"
2) Store in ValuePort from 3
3) Store in PortNum from 11
4) Call cbDOut with BoardNum,PortNum,ValuePort
5) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDOut 
6) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
7) Set Status - Setup to Error String
8) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
9) End If cb Error

10) If/Then x=y with x=Mode y=0 
11) Store in Mode Value from 40
12) End If x=y
13) If/Then x=y with x=Mode y=1 
14) Store in Mode Value from 80
15) End If x=y
16) If/Then x=y with x=Mode y=2 
17) Store in Mode Value from 136
18) End If x=y
19) Calculate BPortValue with mode=Mode Value DACCS=1 LDAC=1 CLR=1 
20) Store in ValuePort from BPortValue
21) Call cbDOut with BoardNum,PortNum,ValuePort
22) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDOut 
23) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
24) Set Status - Setup to Error String

Initialize (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Initialize)

This object does the initialization action, and is cqalled by object Startup in the Master Panel.

If the USB cable becomes disconnected, it should be possible to click on Initialized to restart 
without having to close and start the application over.

Action list for Pushbutton object "Initialize":

1) Store in StopTask from 1
2) Store in RestartFlag from 0
3) Set Charts(Enabled) to 0
4) Set Status - Setup to ""
5) ------------------ -------------------------------------"-S--et Board Num to First Found"
6) Store in BoardNum from 0
7) ------------------ -------------------------------------"-C--ounterNum is the counter number read from the

1408"
8) Store in CounterNum from 1
9) Clear BoardName

10) ------------------ -------------------------------------"-G--et Board"
11) Store in Text message from "Device"
12) Call cbGetBoardName with BoardNum,BoardName
13) If/Then/Else cb Error with X=cbGetBoardName 
14) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
15) Set Status - Setup to Error String
16) Else if not cb Error
17) Store in Serial # from BoardName
18) Append to Text message from Serial #
19) ------------------ -------------------------------------"-T--ext Message not used; instead use custom

message"
20) Set Status - Setup to "Configuration file valid for EC14"
21) Delay Action Delay for 2 seconds
22) ------------------ -------------------------------------"-C--onfigure I/O"
23) Set Status - Setup to "Initializing EC14 I/O"
24) Delay Action Delay for 1 seconds
25) ------------------ -------------------------------------"-C--onfigure Port A to output"
26) Store in PortNum from 10
27) ------------------ -------------------------------------"-D--igitalOut=1, DigitalIn=2"
28) Store in Direction from 1
29) Call cbDConfigPort with BoardNum,PortNum,Direction
30) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDConfigPort 
31) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
32) Set Status - Setup to Error String
33) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
34) End If cb Error
35) ------------------ -------------------------------------"-S--et Port A lines all high"
36) Store in ValuePort from 255
37) Store in PortNum from 10
38) Call cbDOut with BoardNum,PortNum,ValuePort
39) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDOut 
40) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
41) Set Status - Setup to Error String
42) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
43) End If cb Error
44) ------------------ -------------------------------------"-C--onfigure Port B to output"
45) Store in PortNum from 11

Clock Loop (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Gains & SPI\Clock Loop)

Action list for Pushbutton object "Clock Loop":

1) ------------------ ---------SPI "All devices are CS on low, data sample on clk high to low"
2) ------------------ ---------SPI "Initialize CLK=1 and DOUT=1"
3) ------------------ ---------SPI "and all !CS to output=1"
4) Store in CLKFlag from 0
5) Store in ValuePort from 255
6) Store in PortNum from 10
7) Call cbDOut with BoardNum,PortNum,ValuePort
8) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDOut 
9) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String

10) Set Status - Setup to Error String
11) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
12) End If cb Error
13) ------------------ ---------SPI "output data on bit 7, clk is on bit 6"
14) Store in DOut from 7
15) Store in CLK from 6
16) ------------------ ---------SPI "output data on bit 7, clk is on bit 6"
17) Store in DOut from 7
18) Store in CLK from 6
19) ------------------ ---------SPI "Toggle the CLK"
20) Repeat loop ClkLoop until CLKFlag is true (non-zero)
21) Call cbDBitOut with BoardNum,PortNum,CLK,0
22) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDBitOut 
23) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
24) Set Status - Setup to Error String
25) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
26) End If cb Error
27) Call cbDBitOut with BoardNum,PortNum,CLK,1
28) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDBitOut 
29) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
30) Set Status - Setup to Error String
31) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
32) End If cb Error
33) End ClkLoop

SPI (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Gains & SPI\SPI)

Action list takes as inputs
# bytes in %1
and vector of bytes of data in %2
The clock signal is on D6
The serial data line is D7
Chip Select must be done outside the action list, which allows multiple devices to be selected

Action list for Action object "SPI":

1) Store in PortNum from 10
2) ------------------ ---------SPI "output data on bit 7, clk is on bit 6"
3) Store in DOut from 7
4) Store in CLK from 6
5) Linear series ByteLoop from 1 to "%1", step by 1
6) Calculate n-1 with n=ByteLoop 
7) Calculate VectorElement with vect="%2" i=n-1 
8) ------------------ ---------SPI "BitVector reverses the vector so that MSB is output first"
9) Calculate BitVector with x=VectorElement n=8 

10) Linear series BitLoop from 0 to 7, step by 1
11) Calculate BitValue with vect=BitVector i=BitLoop 
12) Call cbDBitOut with BoardNum,PortNum,DOut,BitValue
13) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDBitOut 
14) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
15) Set Status - Setup to Error String
16) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
17) End If cb Error
18) ------------------ ---------SPI "Toggle the CLK, data is read on low to high"
19) Call cbDBitOut with BoardNum,PortNum,CLK,0
20) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDBitOut 
21) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
22) Set Status - Setup to Error String
23) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
24) End If cb Error
25) Call cbDBitOut with BoardNum,PortNum,CLK,1
26) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDBitOut 
27) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
28) Set Status - Setup to Error String
29) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
30) End If cb Error
31) End BitLoop  
32) End ByteLoop  

Settings for Action object "SPI":

Action parameter phrase: bytes= %1 and data vector=%2
Action Name: Run

GainByteVector (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Gains & SPI\GainByteVector)

Settings for Math object "GainByteVector":

Formula: vector(A + (16 * B))

Settings for Math object "vector modify":

Formula: vectorReplace(vector,i,value)

BitVector (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Gains & SPI\BitVector)

This object takes a byte, and creates a bit vector with the MSB as item 0.
ramp creates the vector of 1s
pow2 modifies the vector into a vector of 1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128
x is the byte to be bit wise anded with the above vector
sgn produces a 1 for a positive value
reverse puts the msb first so that the bit vector can be output msb first

Settings for Math object "BitVector":

Formula: reverse(sgn(x and (pow2(ramp(n)))))

BitValue (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Gains & SPI\BitValue)

Settings for Math object "BitValue":

Formula: index(vect,i)

DAC MSB (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Gains & SPI\DAC MSB)

Settings for Math object "DAC MSB":

Formula: Address*64 + INT(y/256)

DAC LSB (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Gains & SPI\DAC LSB)

Settings for Math object "DAC LSB":

Formula: X MOD Y

Set Frequencies (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Frequency\Set Frequencies)

General flow
1) Control the chip select lines of the two DDS chips with EDigitalOut.
2) Write to both DDS chips Freq Registers.
3) Chip select only the Q DDS.
4) Write to DDS chip Q phase register.
5) Chip select both DDS chips.
6) Write to the control registers of both chips ('20H') to release the Reset.

Action list for Pushbutton object "Set Frequencies":

1) ------------------ ---------SPI "All devices are CS on low, data sample on clk high to low"
2) ------------------ ---------SPI "Initialize CLK=1 and DOUT=1"
3) ------------------ ---------SPI "and all !CS to output=1"

5) Store in PortNum from 10

11) End If cb Error

18) ------------------ ---------1 "Initialize 18 bytes with zeros"
19) Calculate vector of zeros with n=18 
20) ------------------ ---------1 "Set D13 (B28) and D8 (Reset)"
21) ------------------ ---------1 "Second byte of control register is all zero"
22) Calculate vector modify with vector=vector of zeros i=0 value=33 
23) ------------------ ---------1 "load next 4 bytes with register Freq0 values "

147) Run SPI bytes= 2 and data vector=Freq0 bytes
148) ------------------ ---------1 "Select OscI"
149) Set BitValue to 0
150) Store in BitNum from 2
151) Call cbDBitOut with BoardNum,PortNum,BitNum,BitValue
152) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDBitOut 
153) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String
154) Set Status - Setup to Error String
155) Delay MessageDelay for 2 seconds
156) End If cb Error
157) ------------------ ---------1 "load vector with Control Register value to release Reset"
158) Calculate vector modify with vector=vector of zeros i=0 value=32 
159) Store in Freq0 bytes from vector modify
160) ------------------ ---------1 "Release Reset"
161) Run SPI bytes= 2 and data vector=Freq0 bytes
162) ------------------ ---------1 "Deselect devices and set clk and data lines high"
163) Call cbDOut with BoardNum,PortNum,ValuePort
164) If/Then cb Error with X=cbDOut 
165) Call cbGetErrMsg with cb Error,Error String

14LSBs (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Frequency\14LSBs)

Settings for Math object "14LSBs":

Formula: x mod y

MSB (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Frequency\MSB)

Control bits D15, D14 specify what register is written to:
00 - control register
01 - freq 0 (14 bits) - add decimal 64 to MSB
10 - freq 1 (14 bits) - add decimal 128 to MSB
If bit D15 and D14 are set, it is a phase register, then D13 specifys which phase register
110X - phase 0 (12 bits) - add decimal 192
111X - phase 1 (12 bits) - add decimal 224

Settings for Math object "MSB":

Formula: int(y/256) + CB

LSB (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Frequency\LSB)

Settings for Math object "LSB":

Formula: x mod y

vector of zeros (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Frequency\vector of zeros)

Settings for Math object "vector of zeros":

Formula: zero(n)

vector modify (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Frequency\vector modify)

Settings for Math object "vector modify":

Formula: vectorReplace(vector,i,value)

Cancel (\EC14 CHARTS\EC14 Setup\Frequency\Cancel)

General flow
1) Control the chip select lines of the two DDS chips with EDigitalOut.
2) Write to both DDS chips Freq Registers.
3) Chip select only the Q DDS.
4) Write to DDS chip Q phase register.
5) Chip select both DDS chips.
6) Write to the control registers of both chips ('20H') to release the Reset.

Action list for Pushbutton object "Cancel":

1) Show EC14 Setup  
2) Hide Frequency

A/D data list 2 (\EC14 CHARTS\Containers\A/D data list 2)

Stores data from A/D object when running in 1XY mode to allow unerased data to be available for scaling and rotation and phase wand.

The data is stored without any scaling or offset changes.  

A/D data list (\EC14 CHARTS\Containers\A/D data list)

Stores data from A/D object for scaling and filtering.  This object
is required because data passed to the real-time strip chart cannot
be accessed again for filtering, archiving etc.

The data is stored without any scaling or offset changes.
